- **file-issue**: File a well-shaped GitHub issue from a short intent — pick the right form (feature / bug / research), fill its sections, infer kind/zone/milestone, and set native dependency edges. Never files an umbrella issue — decomposes into self-contained issues with real blocked-by edges. ([tatolab/streamlib](https://github.com/tatolab/streamlib/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/file-issue) · ★6)

SKIP: cash timing, runway or burn questions -&gt; fin/cashflow-forecast (forecasts cash movement, not spend allocation); full P&L/Balance Sheet projections -&gt; fin/financial-model (3-statement modelling, not budget approval). ([andreagroferreira/arka-os](https://github.com/andreagroferreira/arka-os/tree/HEAD/departments/finance/skills/budget-plan) · ★16)
- **fin/cashflow-forecast**: Cash flow forecasting across operating, investing and financing flows, with runway, burn rate and working-capital analysis (Leonor, Financial Analyst — Cash Flow Management + FP&A). TRIGGER: "cashflow forecast", "previsão de tesouraria", "quanto runway temos", "burn rate", "fluxo de caixa", "/fin cashflow". SKIP: allocating spend by department or headcount -&gt; fin/budget-plan (budgeting, not cash timing); full 3-statement projections -&gt; fin/financial-model (cash flow is one statement of three); stress-testing assumptions best/worst case -&gt; fin/scenario-analysis. ([andreagroferreira/arka-os](https://github.com/andreagroferreira/arka-os/tree/HEAD/departments/finance/skills/cashflow-forecast) · ★16)
- **fin/financial-model**: Builds 3-statement financial models — P&L, Balance Sheet, Cash Flow — with base/optimistic/pessimistic scenarios, following Damodaran methodology; outputs model tables, scenario comparison and a key-metrics dashboard. TRIGGER: "modelo financeiro", "financial model", "projeções a 3 anos", "3-statement model", "modela o P&L", "/fin model". SKIP: company worth or price -&gt; fin/valuation-model (DCF/comps built on top of the model); cash runway only -&gt; fin/cashflow-forecast; standalone what-if sensitivity -&gt; fin/scenario-analysis (stresses assumptions, doesn't build statements). ([andreagroferreira/arka-os](https://github.com/andreagroferreira/arka-os/tree/HEAD/departments/finance/skills/financial-model) · ★16)
- **fin/pitch-deck**: Investor pitch deck financials with Rui (Investment Strategist): market size, traction, unit economics, funding ask, projections and cap-table impact, following the 10-slide Pitch Deck Framework + Venture Deals. TRIGGER: "pitch deck", "slides para investidores", "quanto pedir na ronda", "fundraising deck", "investor deck financials", "/fin pitch". SKIP: computing the underlying CAC/LTV numbers -&gt; fin/unit-economics (this skill presents them); defending a valuation number -&gt; fin/valuation-model; building the projection model behind the slides -&gt; fin/financial-model. ([andreagroferreira/arka-os](https://github.com/andreagroferreira/arka-os/tree/HEAD/departments/finance/skills/pitch-deck) · ★16)
- **fin/scenario-analysis**: Financial scenario analysis: base, optimistic and pessimistic cases with key-variable sensitivity and probability weighting (Monte Carlo + Scenario Analysis, Leonor). TRIGGER: "análise de cenários", "e se as vendas caírem 20%", "scenario analysis", "sensitivity analysis", "worst case financeiro", "/fin scenario". SKIP: building the underlying statements first -&gt; fin/financial-model (scenarios stress an existing model); strategic/market futures rather than financial numbers -&gt; strategy/scenario-plan (business scenarios, not P&L sensitivity). ([andreagroferreira/arka-os](https://github.com/andreagroferreira/arka-os/tree/HEAD/departments/finance/skills/scenario-analysis) · ★16)
- **fin/unit-economics**: Unit economics analysis: CAC, LTV, LTV:CAC, payback, Rule of 40, NRR, burn multiple and magic number, benchmarked against KeyBanc/Meritech private-SaaS quartiles, with a traffic-light dashboard and improvement recommendations. TRIGGER: "unit economics", "qual é o nosso CAC", "LTV to CAC", "payback period", "Rule of 40", "/fin unit-economics". SKIP: ongoing KPI tracking and dashboard build -&gt; saas/metrics-dashboard (operational dashboard, not one-off diagnosis); fixing churn drivers behind weak NRR -&gt; saas/churn-analysis; setting price points -&gt; saas/pricing-strategy. ([andreagroferreira/arka-os](https://github.com/andreagroferreira/arka-os/tree/HEAD/departments/finance/skills/unit-economics) · ★16)
- **fin/valuation-model**: Company valuation: DCF with WACC (Damodaran), comparable company analysis and precedent transactions, producing a valuation range with multiples and sensitivity analysis. TRIGGER: "quanto vale a empresa", "valuation", "avaliação da empresa", "DCF", "múltiplos comparáveis", "/fin valuation". SKIP: building the projections the DCF discounts -&gt; fin/financial-model (valuation consumes its outputs); designing the business model itself -&gt; strategy/bmc (Business Model Canvas, not enterprise value); presenting the number to investors -&gt; fin/pitch-deck. ([andreagroferreira/arka-os](https://github.com/andreagroferreira/arka-os/tree/HEAD/departments/finance/skills/valuation-model) · ★16)
